**[00:32:23.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000734)

**[00:32:25.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000735)

**[00:32:28.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000736)

**[00:32:30.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000737)

**[00:32:34.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000739)

Prior authorization

Ito po ay mga naitanong na natin

**[00:51:25.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001252)  
at nagpapahayag mo ng pasasalamat

**[00:51:27.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001253)  
ang representasyon na ito

**[00:51:28.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001254)

**[00:51:33.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001255)  
at gusto rin po nating mag-follow up.

**[00:51:36.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001256)  
Gaya po, for example,

**[00:51:38.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001257)

**[00:51:42.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001258)  
ang paggamit nito during police operations.

**[00:51:45.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001259)  
Pwede po ba nating matanong,

**[00:51:47.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001260)

**[00:51:49.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001261)

**[00:51:53.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001262)  
Kung ganun po,

**[00:51:54.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001263)

**[00:51:57.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001264)

**[00:51:59.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001265)  
tuwing may mobilisasyon?

**[00:52:01.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001266)  
Actually, sad to say, Mr. Speaker,

**[00:52:03.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001267)

**[00:52:08.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001268)

**[00:52:10.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001269)  
Pero ang kinaganda po,

**[00:52:11.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001270)

**[00:52:14.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001271)

**[00:52:17.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001272)  
But because of budget constraint,

**[00:52:22.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001273)

**[00:52:25.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001274)  
At ginagamit po ito

**[00:52:26.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001275)

**[00:52:27.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001276)

**[00:52:29.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001277)

**[00:52:31.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001278)

**[00:52:33.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001279)

**[00:52:35.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001280)

**[00:52:39.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001282)

**[00:52:42.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001283)

**[00:52:44.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001284)

**[00:52:53.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001286)

**[00:52:54.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001287)

**[00:52:56.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001288)

Sa deployment of police officers po,

**[00:53:08.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001291)

**[00:53:10.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001292)

**[00:53:12.680] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001293)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[00:53:14.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001294)  
Ah, sige.

**[00:53:15.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001295)

**[00:53:18.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001296)  
Ukol nga rin po sa specifications and standards

**[00:53:20.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001297)

**[00:53:23.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001298)

**[00:53:26.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001299)

**[00:53:30.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001300)

**[00:53:32.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001301)

**[00:53:36.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001302)

**[00:53:38.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001303)

**[00:53:40.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001304)

**[00:53:43.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001305)

**[00:53:45.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001306)  
Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[00:53:49.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001307)  
Actually, Mr. Speaker,

**[00:53:51.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001308)

**[00:53:53.220] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001309)

**[00:53:56.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001310)

**[00:54:00.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001311)

**[00:54:03.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001312)

**[00:54:06.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001313)  
So, yung sinumiti po bang Memo Circular 2024

**[00:54:10.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001314)

**[00:54:13.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001315)

**[00:54:14.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001316)  
Dahil nga po, as mentioned,

**[00:54:16.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001317)

**[00:54:17.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001318)

**[00:54:22.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001319)

**[00:54:23.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001320)  
pero ina-update po natin ito

**[00:54:25.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001321)

**[00:54:32.210] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001322)

**[00:54:37.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001323)

**[00:54:40.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001324)

**[00:54:42.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001325)  
but it's being

**[00:54:44.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001326)

**[00:54:47.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001327)

**[00:54:48.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001328)

**[00:54:51.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001329)

Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[00:54:59.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001333)

**[00:55:32.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001350)

**[00:55:34.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001351)

**[00:55:35.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001352)

**[00:55:37.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001353)

**[00:55:42.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001354)

**[00:56:17.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001373)

**[00:56:20.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001374)  
Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[00:57:16.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001402)

**[00:57:20.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001403)

**[00:57:22.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001404)

**[00:57:32.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001408)

**[00:57:36.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001410)

**[00:57:38.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001411)

**[00:57:40.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001412)

**[00:57:42.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001413)  
Chief PNP at ng ating

NAPOLCOM under the supervision

Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[00:58:02.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001422)  
nag-prepare ang kapulisan

**[00:58:04.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001423)

**[00:58:09.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001425)

**[00:58:27.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001432)  
So, Mr. Speaker, good sponsor,

**[00:58:29.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001433)

**[01:05:00.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001533)

**[01:05:11.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001535)

**[01:05:13.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001536)

**[01:05:16.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001537)

**[01:05:32.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001541)

**[01:05:34.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001542)

**[01:05:38.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001543)  
Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[01:05:44.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001545)

**[01:05:46.600] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001546)

**[01:05:48.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001547)  
Yes po, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:05:50.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001548)  
And just like in the past po, we express our deep concern in responding to the crisis of school shooting.

**[01:05:59.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001549)

**[01:06:08.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001550)

**[01:06:17.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001551)

**[01:06:26.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001552)  
So as a lawyer, I'd like to make it clear.

**[01:06:28.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001553)  
The violation of...

**[01:06:29.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001554)  
The violation of a person's rights will not solve school violence.

**[01:06:33.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001555)  
This representation can think of action steps it must first prioritize to ensure children do not commit school violence.

**[01:06:39.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001556)

**[01:06:46.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001557)

**[01:06:53.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001558)  
Stricter gun policies and monitoring for government employees with guns.

**[01:06:57.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001559)  
Given that the recent school shootings...

**[01:06:59.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001560)  
Result of children na may access po sa guns ng parents and guardians na sila po ay members ng state forces or government employees.

**[01:07:09.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001561)  
So, Mr. Speaker, good sponsor, sa mga huli po natin sigurong tanong, pero ang una nating gustong i-raise ay na-raise po natin sa Dangerous Drugs Board.

**[01:07:20.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001562)

**[01:07:59.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001566)

**[01:08:05.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001567)

**[01:08:36.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001571)

**[01:08:47.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001572)

**[01:08:50.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001573)  
But, is it enough? I don't think so.

**[01:08:53.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001574)  
We have to do more.

**[01:08:54.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001575)  
So, we have to think of more ways.

**[01:08:58.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001576)  
Especially kung paano...

**[01:08:59.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001577)

**[01:09:03.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001578)

**[01:09:10.480] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001579)

**[01:09:17.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001580)

**[01:09:23.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001581)

**[01:09:29.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001582)

**[01:09:31.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001583)

**[01:09:45.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001585)

Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[01:11:15.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001600)

**[01:11:29.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001602)

**[01:11:51.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001605)  
Yes, thank you for that question.

**[01:11:54.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001606)

**[01:12:02.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001608)

**[01:12:06.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001609)  
But the problem is PIDEA, do they have sufficient men? No.

**[01:12:11.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001610)  
So, minsan nga po, kailangan po deputize.

**[01:12:14.480] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001611)  
May mga operational na ginagawa na po ang PIDEA.

**[01:12:17.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001612)  
Paano po padaliin ang pag-operate rin ng ating kapulisan?

**[01:12:21.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001613)  
Dahil very frustrating rin po sa ating kapulisan, lalo na pag may nahuli regarding drugs,

**[01:12:26.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001614)  
because of certain technicalities,

**[01:12:28.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001615)  
na hindi po nabigay ang papel ng PIDEA, etc.,

**[01:12:31.820] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001616)  
nadidismiss po yung ating kaso.

**[01:12:34.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001617)  
So, we recognize the problem, but the DILG is committed na mas maganda po sigurong pag-usapan pa lalo ng DDB,

**[01:12:43.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001618)  
although wala po talagang directang mandato ang DILG doon,

**[01:12:49.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001619)  
but since they are handling the police, so tama po kayo na dapat mas maganda yung pag-uusap at continuing on how to improve,

**[01:12:57.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001620)  
lalo na ang ating operation regarding illegal drugs.

**[01:13:01.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001621)  
But one thing is for sure, hindi po natin kailangan na pumatay ng tao para po masugpo ang problema sa droga ng ating pong bansa.

**[01:13:12.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001622)  
Ang huling tanong na lang po natin, Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[01:13:16.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001623)  
Kapansin-pansin sa mga POPs ng mga LGU, yung pinupondohang Barangay Intelligence Networks.

**[01:13:26.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001624)  
Ito po yung isa sa mga POPs ng mga LGU, yung pinupondohang Barangay Intelligence Networks.

**[01:13:26.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001625)  
Ito po yung isa sa mga POPs ng mga LGU, yung pinupondohang Barangay Intelligence Networks.

**[01:13:27.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001626)  
Ito po yung isa sa mga pinaka-heavily-funded items under anti-drugs POPs plans.

**[01:13:35.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001627)  
At kasama po dito ang mga aktividad gaya ng recruiting informants, maintaining safe houses, identifying high-value and street-level individuals.

**[01:13:45.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001628)  
Ngunit wala pa tayong nakikitang specific issuance na nag-establish o nagbibigay ng regulasyon tungkol po sa pagkakaroon ng Barangay Intelligence Networks.

**[01:13:54.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001629)  
Line item lang po siya sa mga LGU POPs plans.

**[01:13:57.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001630)  
So ang tanong po natin, Mr. Speaker, dahil napupondohan na ang Barangay Intelligence Networks for years now, at scale, meron po bang specific DILG or national issuance na may numero na maaari pong makahingi ang representasyon ng kopya na ito that authorizes and governs these Barangay Intelligence Networks?

**[01:14:17.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001631)  
At sa anong otoridad po ba ito?

**[01:14:20.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001632)  
Now oversee yung informant recruitment, safe house use.

**[01:14:26.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001633)  
Kapag...

**[01:14:26.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001634)  
Nag-gather po ito, chinecheck kung effective yung paglabas po ng pondo, etc.

**[01:14:33.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001635)  
Thank you for that question, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:14:35.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001636)  
That's between the PNP and the LGU.

**[01:14:38.220] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001637)  
So on DILG po, has nothing to do about it.

**[01:14:41.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001638)  
So wala pong DILG issuance or national issuance na nag-govern po?

**[01:14:45.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001639)  
I would like to remind, honorable ko, none, it's okay ha, none, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:14:50.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001640)  
Madam, Mr. Speaker, good sponsor, just to wrap it up.

**[01:14:54.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001641)  
Dahil napansin natin na...

**[01:14:56.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001642)  
Napakat laki ng pondo.

**[01:15:00.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001643)  
Sa Barangay Intelligence Networks, under POP's plans,

**[01:15:04.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001644)  
na kahit sinasabi natin nagbabago or nag-evolve continuously,

**[01:15:09.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001645)  
pero ang DDB na mismo yung nagsabi na inaabandonan na ito dati

**[01:15:13.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001646)  
at ginagawa na yung bago, may malaking mismatch po talaga

**[01:15:17.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001647)  
doon sa paano nabibitbit sa baba ang mga ibang-ibang plano

**[01:15:26.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001648)  
na gustong i-implementa sa taas, na nakakadulot ng totoong harm,

**[01:15:32.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001649)  
lalo na sa mga biktima ng gera kontra droga,

**[01:15:35.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001650)  
ng kanilang mga pamilya na hanggang ngayon di pa rin po nakaka-recover

**[01:15:38.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001651)  
totally, Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[01:15:40.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001652)  
I have many other questions but I have run out of time, Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[01:15:45.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001653)  
I will just simply conduct other questions via dialogue,

**[01:15:51.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001654)  
but for now, there being no other questions,

**[01:15:55.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001655)  
from the minority, I move to terminate the deliberation of the budget of the DILG

**[01:16:04.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001656)  
and other attached agencies and corporations.

**[01:16:07.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001657)  
So move, Mr. Chair.

**[01:16:09.160] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-001658)  
What does the House say?

**[01:16:10.700] Floor Speaker** (asr-001659)  
Mr. Speaker, on the part of the majority,

**[01:16:13.300] Floor Speaker** (asr-001660)  
we join the minority in their motion to terminate the budget

**[01:16:16.860] Floor Speaker** (asr-001661)  
of the Department of Interior and Local Government, including its attached agencies.

**[01:16:22.820] Floor Speaker** (asr-001662)  
So move.

**[01:16:23.240] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-001663)  
There is a joint motion.

**[01:16:25.600] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-001664)  
To terminate the interpellation on the proposed budget of the Department of the Interior and Local Government,

**[01:16:33.840] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-001665)  
including attached agencies.

**[01:16:36.100] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-001666)  
There is no objection.

**[01:16:38.240] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-001667)  
Hearing none, the interpellation on the proposed budget of the Department of the Interior and Local Government,

**[01:16:45.920] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-001668)  
including attached agencies, is hereby terminated.

**[01:16:51.780] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-001669)  
Majority Leader.

**[01:16:55.600] Floor Speaker** (asr-001672)  
Mr. Speaker, move to suspend session.

**[01:16:57.780] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-001673)  
Session is suspended.

**[01:20:54.590] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001681)  
Mr. Speaker, I now move to consider the proposed budget of the Department of Tourism and its agencies.

**[01:20:59.480] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001682)  
There is a motion to consider the budget of the Department of Tourism, including attached agencies and corporations.

**[01:21:07.600] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001683)  
Any objection? None. Motion is carried. Majority Leader.

**[01:21:11.440] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001684)  
Mr. Speaker, may we acknowledge the presence of the heads and officials from the Department of Tourism, namely:

**[01:21:17.960] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001685)  
Secretary [unclear], Undersecretary Stanley Ng,

**[01:21:23.120] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001686)  
Undersecretary Maria Dionne Rivera Guillermo,

**[01:21:25.840] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001688)

**[01:21:29.120] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001689)

**[01:21:33.380] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001690)  
Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin.

**[01:21:36.040] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001691)  
Administrator-Attorney Joanne Padilla of Intramuros Administration.

**[01:21:40.640] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001692)  
Executive Director Cecile Lorenzana, National Parks Development Committee.

**[01:21:45.600] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001693)  
Executive Director Marco Angelo Acheta, Philippine Commission on Sports.

**[01:21:49.980] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001694)  
Dr. Mark Lapid, Chief Operating Officer, Tourism Infrastructure and Enterprise Zone,

**[01:21:55.580] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001695)  
Authority, Chief Operating Officer, Maria Margarita Montemayor Nograles Tourism Promotions Board,

**[01:22:02.180] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001696)  
Chief Operating Officer, Patrick Joel Cinco, Duty Free Philippines Corporation,

**[01:22:07.540] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001697)  
General Manager Roberto Zozobrado, Philippine Retirement Authority,

**[01:22:12.180] Rep. Ronald V. Singson** (asr-001698)  
Executive Director Leonila Fabul of Nayong Pilipino Foundation.

**[01:22:17.080] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001699)  
In behalf of Speaker Boji D. and Majority Leader Sandro Marcos,

**[01:22:24.500] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001700)  
we welcome the...

**[01:22:25.580] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001701)  
D.O.T. heads and officials to the House of Representatives.

**[01:22:30.840] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001702)  
Majority Leader.

**[01:22:33.980] Rep. De Carlo L. Uy** (asr-001703)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to recognize Representative Marie Bernadette G. Escudero,

**[01:22:41.920] Rep. De Carlo L. Uy** (asr-001704)  
Vice Chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations,

**[01:22:45.840] Rep. De Carlo L. Uy** (asr-001705)  
to answer questions on the proposed budget of the Department of Tourism.

**[01:22:50.480] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001706)  
The gentlelady from the 1st District, Sor Sogon,

**[01:22:54.340] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001707)  
Vice Chair on...

**[01:22:55.580] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001708)  
The Honorable Maria Bernadette Escudero.

**[01:22:58.580] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001709)  
Ma'am, you may start.

**[01:23:01.440] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001710)  
Salamat, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:23:03.900] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001711)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleagues,

**[01:23:06.700] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001712)  
I rise today to sponsor the proposed budget of the Department of Tourism

**[01:23:10.640] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001713)  
and its attached agencies for fiscal year 2027.

**[01:23:15.860] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001714)  
Tourism is an investment that multiplies.

**[01:23:18.840] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001715)  
The government may provide the first peso,

**[01:23:21.320] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001716)  
but tourism allows that peso to move through our economy.

**[01:23:25.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001717)  
Umiikot mula sa airport, papunta sa hotel, restaurants, tour guides, transport operators,

**[01:23:33.780] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001718)  
magsasaka, souvenir shops at maliliit na negosyo.

**[01:23:37.780] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001719)  
And along the way, that one peso becomes income, jobs, and livelihoods for millions of Filipinos.

**[01:23:44.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001720)  
We already see this happening across our economy.

**[01:23:48.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001721)  
In 2025, tourism directly generated 2.27 trillion in economic value,

**[01:23:54.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001722)  
equivalent to 8.1% of our GDP.

**[01:23:59.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001723)  
Domestic travelers spent 3.26 trillion,

**[01:24:04.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001724)  
while international visitors brought approximately 698 billion into our economy.

**[01:24:11.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001725)  
Tourism also supported 7.7 million jobs,

**[01:24:15.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001726)  
or 15.7% of total employment in the Philippines.

**[01:24:20.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001727)  
Mr. Speaker, behind those numbers are people,

**[01:24:23.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001728)  
the hotel employee

**[01:24:24.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001729)  
who depends on a steady flow of guests,

**[01:24:27.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001730)  
the tour guide waiting for the next group of visitors,

**[01:24:30.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001731)  
the restaurant owner, the driver, the souvenir maker,

**[01:24:34.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001732)  
and the small business owner.

**[01:24:36.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001733)  
They tell us something very important.

**[01:24:38.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001734)  
When we invest in tourism,

**[01:24:40.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001735)  
the benefits do not stay within the tourism sector.

**[01:24:43.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001736)  
They spread across our economy.

**[01:24:46.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001737)  
Mr. Speaker, dear colleagues,

**[01:24:48.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001738)  
at the 2025 World Travel Awards,

**[01:24:51.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001739)  
the Philippines was named Asia's leading beach destination.

**[01:24:53.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001740)  
Asia's leading dive destination.

**[01:24:57.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001741)  
And Asia's leading island destination.

**[01:25:00.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001742)  
It was our seventh consecutive year as Asia's leading dive destination

**[01:25:05.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001743)  
and our eighth recognition as Asia's leading beach destination.

**[01:25:10.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001744)  
Our appeal also goes beyond leisure travel.

**[01:25:13.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001745)  
The Philippines was recognized as Asia's best retirement destination in 2025.

**[01:25:18.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001746)  
And this year was named world's number one retirement destination

**[01:25:22.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001747)  
for 2026 in the Retirement Abroad Index.

**[01:25:27.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001748)  
So the challenge is not whether Philippine tourism has value.

**[01:25:32.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001749)  
The challenge is unlocking its full scale.

**[01:25:35.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001750)  
And promotion is central to that effort.

**[01:25:38.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001751)  
Building on the Love the Philippines campaign,

**[01:25:41.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001752)  
the department has introduced Discover More to Love,

**[01:25:45.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001753)  
reinforcing our existing brand while encouraging travelers

**[01:25:48.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001754)  
to discover more of our destinations,

**[01:25:51.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001755)  
experiences, culture, and people.

**[01:25:54.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001756)  
Mr. Speaker, the objective is conversion.

**[01:25:57.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001757)  
From seeing the Philippines,

**[01:25:59.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001758)  
to wanting to visit the Philippines,

**[01:26:02.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001759)  
to actually booking a trip to the Philippines.

**[01:26:05.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001760)  
This objective requires the resources to reach the markets we are competing for.

**[01:26:10.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001761)  
For fiscal year 2027,

**[01:26:12.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001762)  
the National Expenditure Program proposes approximately 4 billion

**[01:26:17.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001763)  
of the Department of Tourism and its attached agencies,

**[01:26:20.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001764)  
around 196 million lower than the fiscal year 2026 General Appropriations Act.

**[01:26:27.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001765)  
This includes 1 billion for the Global Branding Campaign

**[01:26:30.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001766)  
together with approximately 1.37 billion budget

**[01:26:34.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001767)  
of the Tourism Promotions Board.

**[01:26:37.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001768)  
Our resources for tourism promotions remain far smaller

**[01:26:41.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001769)  
than those of several of our major ASEAN competitors.

**[01:26:45.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001770)  
Available comparative figures put Philippine tourism marketing spending

**[01:26:48.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001771)  
at around 86 million U.S. dollars,

**[01:26:51.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001772)  
compared with approximately 499 million U.S. dollars for Thailand,

**[01:26:56.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001773)  
418 million for Singapore,

**[01:26:59.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001774)  
and 331 million U.S. dollars for Malaysia.

**[01:27:02.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001775)  
Mr. Speaker, we are competing for the same international travelers.

**[01:27:07.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001776)  
Every destination is trying to capture their attention,

**[01:27:10.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001777)  
convince them to book, and persuade them to stay and spend.

**[01:27:14.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001778)  
If we want a greater share of that market,

**[01:27:17.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001779)  
we must be prepared to compete for it.

**[01:27:20.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001780)  
Promotion, however, is only one part of the effort.

**[01:27:23.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001781)  
Tourism works best when government works together.

**[01:27:27.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001782)  
We want visitors to arrive easily, move around efficiently,

**[01:27:32.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001783)  
discover more destinations, and leave wanting to come back again.

**[01:27:36.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001784)  
That requires a whole-of-government effort,

**[01:27:39.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001785)  
because visitors do not experience our agencies separately.

**[01:27:43.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001786)  
They simply experience the Philippines.

**[01:27:46.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001787)  
Given limited fiscal space,

**[01:27:48.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001788)  
additional resources must be targeted where they can make the greatest difference.

**[01:27:52.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001789)  
Scuba diving is one clear example.

**[01:27:55.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001790)  
Our dive tourism industry is estimated at 1.3 billion U.S. dollars.

**[01:28:00.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001791)  
We have 29 dive destination clusters, 956 identified dive sites,

**[01:28:05.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001792)  
and we have been recognized as Asia's leading dive destination

**[01:28:09.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001793)  
for seven consecutive years.

**[01:28:11.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001794)  
An additional 82.49 million for the Philippine Commission on Sports Scuba Diving

**[01:28:15.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001795)  
would support the development and promotion of dive tourism,

**[01:28:20.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001796)  
improve data monitoring, and strengthen emergency response capabilities.

**[01:28:25.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001797)  
This is the kind of targeted investment that can reach beyond the tourism activity itself

**[01:28:30.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001798)  
to resorts, dive professionals, transport operators,

**[01:28:34.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001799)  
restaurants, and businesses in our coastal communities.

**[01:28:38.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001800)  
One tourism product can support an entire local economy.

**[01:28:42.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001801)  
The same principle applies to our cultural heritage.

**[01:28:45.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001802)  
Intramuros is one such opportunity.

**[01:28:48.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001803)  
Next year, the Intramuros Administration intends to advance its effort again

**[01:28:52.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001804)  
toward UNESCO World Heritage Recognition

**[01:28:55.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001805)  
and is seeking additional funding support for the work needed

**[01:28:59.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001806)  
to strengthen the walled city's conservation, management,

**[01:29:02.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001807)  
and readiness for the UNESCO process.

**[01:29:05.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001808)  
Tourism development is not always about building something new.

**[01:29:09.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001809)  
Sometimes it is about protecting what we already have

**[01:29:12.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001810)  
and allowing the world to experience it.

**[01:29:15.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001811)  
The industry already contributes 2.27 trillion directly to our economy.

**[01:29:21.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001812)  
It already supports 7.7 million Filipino jobs.

**[01:29:25.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001813)  
Our tourism products are already receiving international recognition.

**[01:29:30.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001814)  
The case before us is not whether tourism matters.

**[01:29:33.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001815)  
The numbers have already answered that.

**[01:29:36.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001816)  
The question is how much more value we can create from the tourism economy

**[01:29:40.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001817)  
and how much of that value can reach our people.

**[01:29:44.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001818)  
And that is why I respectfully urge this Chamber

**[01:29:47.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001819)  
to support the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget

**[01:29:51.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001820)  
of the Department of Tourism and its attached agencies

**[01:29:54.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001821)  
and to seriously consider the necessary augmentation for its programs.

**[01:30:00.000] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001822)  
Let us give Philippine tourism the resources to compete.

**[01:30:03.500] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001823)  
Let us give more travelers a reason to love the Philippines.

**[01:30:07.440] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001824)  
And when they come, more reasons to discover, more to love.

**[01:30:11.680] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001825)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Speaker, esteemed colleagues.

**[01:30:17.220] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001826)  
Thank you, Vice Chair Escudero, Majority Leader.

**[01:30:19.740] Rep. De Carlo L. Uy** (asr-001827)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to recognize Representative Iris Marie de Mesa Montes of Partylist 4K to interpolate the sponsor on the proposed budget.

**[01:30:33.980] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001828)  
The gentlelady from 4K, Partylist Representative Iris Marie de Mesa Montes is recognized. Please proceed.

**[01:30:40.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001829)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:30:42.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001830)  
Mr. Chair, distinguished colleague,

**[01:30:45.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001831)  
While the Philippines boasts of world-class destinations,

**[01:30:49.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001832)  
tourism arrivals remain far below potential compared to our ASEAN neighbors.

**[01:30:57.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001833)  
May we ask the department exactly what are the specific challenges that prevent us from maximizing arrivals

**[01:31:05.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001834)  
and what concrete strategies are funded in the 2027 budget to overcome these bottlenecks?

**[01:31:14.120] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001835)  
Salamat po sa tanong, distinguished colleague.

**[01:31:17.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001836)  
Before I begin po, let me...

**[01:31:19.740] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001837)  
share the numbers, no?

**[01:31:22.680] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001838)  
From 2023 up to 2025,

**[01:31:25.860] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001839)  
nag-increase po ng 18.4% ang tourist arrivals natin.

**[01:31:29.960] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001840)  
From 2023, 5.477 million.

**[01:31:34.600] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001841)  
Ngayong 2025 ay 6.484 million.

**[01:31:39.840] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001842)  
So kahit pa pano po, tumaas ng 18.4%.

**[01:31:43.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001843)  
And it is also notable po, no?

**[01:31:49.740] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001844)  
Distinguished colleague,

**[01:31:51.960] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001845)  
na from 2023 also,

**[01:31:54.380] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001846)  
number 6 po tayo in ranking sa ASEAN.

**[01:31:58.340] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001847)  
At noong 2024, bumaba ng konti, naging number 7.

**[01:32:01.820] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001848)  
Pero noong 2025, umangat uli tayo sa number 6,

**[01:32:07.060] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001849)  
na may 6.4 million tourist arrivals.

**[01:32:09.840] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001850)  
Number 1 po is Thailand, number 2, Malaysia, number 3, Vietnam,

**[01:32:13.780] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001851)  
number 4, Singapore, number 5, Indonesia.

**[01:32:17.280] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001852)  
But it is also notable,

**[01:32:19.100] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001853)  
um,

**[01:32:19.740] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001854)  
distinguished colleague,

**[01:32:21.260] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001855)  
na pagdating po sa marketing budget,

**[01:32:24.160] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001856)  
medyo maliit po talaga yung nabigay sa ating departamento.

**[01:32:28.040] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001857)  
Ang budget po natin for 2025 ay 86.68 million dollars.

**[01:32:34.440] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001858)  
As compared po to 499 million for Thailand,

**[01:32:40.520] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001859)  
418 million for Singapore,

**[01:32:42.620] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001860)  
and 331 million for Malaysia.

**[01:32:46.880] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001861)  
So, um, to be blunt po, talagang,

**[01:32:49.740] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001862)  
medyo nahuhuli po tayo sa ASEAN,

**[01:32:51.840] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001863)  
kasi tayo din po yung isa sa may pinakamababang budget po for the department.

**[01:32:59.100] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001864)  
And, um, it is also notable po na kahit mas mababa,

**[01:33:03.000] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001865)  
mas mababa pa yung budget natin,

**[01:33:05.780] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001866)  
kinumpute po natin yung arrivals per million, no?

**[01:33:09.080] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001867)  
Base po dun sa gastos.

**[01:33:11.240] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001868)  
And, um, based on arrivals per million,

**[01:33:13.920] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001869)  
makikita po natin na we have 74,758 arrivals

**[01:33:19.740] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001870)  
tourist arrivals per 1 million na ginastos.

**[01:33:23.080] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001871)  
As compared po to Thailand,

**[01:33:25.680] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001872)  
which has 66,053 arrivals per 1 million na ginastos.

**[01:33:33.780] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001873)  
So, kaya po ganun kalaki din yung naging tourist arrivals nila

**[01:33:37.400] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001874)  
dahil napakalaki din po yung in-invest nila sa promotions.

**[01:33:41.500] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001875)  
Gagaya nga ng sinabi ko kanina sa sponsorship speech ko,

**[01:33:45.260] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001876)  
isa lang po yung pinag-aagawan natin ng mga turista.

**[01:33:48.160] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001877)  
So, kung sino talaga,

**[01:33:49.700] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001878)  
yung mas may malaking promotion budget,

**[01:33:52.780] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001879)  
talagang mas nae-engganyo yung mga turista na pumunta sa kanila.

**[01:33:56.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001880)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:33:59.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001881)  
Tungkol naman po dun sa abolition ng travel tax.

**[01:34:04.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001882)  
Naipasa na po ng Kongreso yung panukalang batas to abolish the travel tax.

**[01:34:10.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001883)  
So, mawawalan po ng funding stream yung TIEZA projects, ano?

**[01:34:16.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001884)  
Malaki po ba ang epekto nito sa ating tourism?

**[01:34:19.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001885)  
At kung sakali po, paano po mautugunan ng ahensya kung sakaling mawalan na ng funding stream yung TIEZA

**[01:34:32.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001886)  
galing dito sa travel tax abolition?

**[01:34:37.620] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001887)  
Madam, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[01:34:42.760] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001888)  
for year 2026, existing pa po yung travel tax collections.

**[01:34:46.740] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001889)  
Dito po sa lower house, napasa na po.

**[01:34:49.700] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001890)  
On third reading, pero as we speak po, pending pa po ang consideration in the Senate.

**[01:34:55.820] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001891)  
So, as of now, mapupondahan po yung mga projects natin under TIEZA.

**[01:35:04.080] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001892)  
To give an idea po, for 2025, 4.4 billion po yung pondo for TIEZA.

**[01:35:10.820] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001893)  
But, if abolished po yung House Bill po natin na 8464, as approved on third reading,

**[01:35:18.520] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001894)  
sinabi naman po na kung hindi na po makakakolekta ng travel tax, which will fund TIEZA.

**[01:35:24.920] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001895)  
Actually, hindi lang po TIEZA, kasama po doon ang CHED and NCCA, pupondohan po ito through the GAA.

**[01:35:31.260] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001896)  
Okay. Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:35:33.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001897)  
Yung susunod naman po tungkol sa Tourism Road Infrastructure Program.

**[01:35:38.280] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001898)  
Ito po, alam naman natin, ito yung Convergence Program with DPWH,

**[01:35:41.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001899)  
which is actually cornerstone for access roads and tourism infrastructure.

**[01:35:47.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001900)  
Meron po ba tayong...

**[01:35:48.500] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001901)  
Ang alokasyon sa 2027 para dito sa mga proyektong ito?

**[01:35:54.260] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001902)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, to be blunt po, zero po.

**[01:35:59.240] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001903)  
Zero po yung allocation natin for the TRIP program.

**[01:36:04.780] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001904)  
Base po sa records, no, simula po 2025, hindi na po nagkaroon ng pondo for new TRIP programs.

**[01:36:13.200] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001905)  
Lahat po yung continuing.

**[01:36:15.760] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001906)  
Ganon din po noong 2026.

**[01:36:17.720] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001907)  
Continuing.

**[01:36:18.500] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001908)  
Programs na lang po for the TRIP.

**[01:36:20.060] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001909)  
Wala din pong new programs.

**[01:36:22.660] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001910)  
And for 2027, both new and continuing po ay zero po ang pondo.

**[01:36:30.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001911)  
Mr. Speaker, again, ano po kaya ang magiging epekto na naman nito sa ating tourism industry?

**[01:36:37.940] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001912)  
Sa totoo lang po, napakalaki po yung magiging epekto nito.

**[01:36:41.080] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001913)  
Dahil ang pinorposed po na budget ng DOT ay 28 projects po amounting to 6 billion.

**[01:36:47.700] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001914)  
For year 2027.

**[01:36:50.060] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001915)  
So, hindi po talaga alam ng department kung saan kukunin tong pondong ito.

**[01:36:55.480] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001916)  
Sinubmit po nila ito sa DPWH and also sa DBM.

**[01:36:59.220] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001917)  
But unfortunately, dahil nga po kulang ng fiscal space, hindi po na provide yung budget na nire-request.

**[01:37:06.060] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001918)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:37:08.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001919)  
Ano naman po ang portion sa 2027 budget natin na allocated for digital tourism, marketing, and online platforms?

**[01:37:17.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001920)  
Given that global travelers now rely on digital channels for destination choices.

**[01:37:29.780] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001921)  
Yung bago pong campaign ng DOT, yung Discover More to Love, it is a reinforcement initiative under the Love the Philippines campaign.

**[01:37:38.960] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001922)  
It is actually an online platform where the government partners with the private sector such as airlines, hotels, and tour operators

**[01:37:45.120] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001923)  
to offer travel deals.

**[01:37:47.020] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001924)  
and discounts, and encourage more people to travel around the country.

**[01:37:50.800] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001925)  
So, in just a few months po, the website has already sold 203,000 airline seats and 800 room nights.

**[01:38:00.440] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001926)  
Mr. Speaker, it actually helps bring in travelers, generate bookings, and support our tourism businesses.

**[01:38:05.900] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001927)  
Especially at this time na tumaas po yung fuel cost, kaya medyo mas bumaba talaga yung turismo sa atin.

**[01:38:11.820] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001928)  
But beyond this po, the Discover More to Love, expound ko na lang din.

**[01:38:17.020] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001929)  
Since we're at it, the DOT has also tapped BINI and SB19, creating dedicated microsites for them

**[01:38:24.240] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001930)  
where fans can explore Philippine destinations through the artists they follow and admire.

**[01:38:31.160] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001931)  
Malaki po at maganda po yung response.

**[01:38:33.480] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001932)  
In just two weeks after the microsites were launched, the DOT website where these microsites are hosted recorded 44,000 new users.

**[01:38:42.800] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001933)  
So, hopefully, magta-translate din to more visitors.

**[01:38:47.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001934)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:38:50.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001935)  
Huling tanong na lamang po, tungkol naman po ito sa tourism workforce training.

**[01:38:56.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001936)  
Ano naman po ang mga programa? What programs are funded in the 2027 budget to upscale tourism workers?

**[01:39:04.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001937)  
Yung guides, hospitality, staff, and local entrepreneurs.

**[01:39:08.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001938)  
So, service quality matches global standards.

**[01:39:17.020] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001939)  
So, as mentioned kanina in my sponsorship speech, meron po tayong 7.7 million employed Filipinos na tinutulungan ng Department of Tourism.

**[01:39:41.340] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001940)  
Under accommodation and food and beverage,

**[01:39:45.080] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001941)  
ang total employment po natin ay...

**[01:39:47.020] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001942)  
2,907,000.

**[01:39:56.220] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001943)  
Isa po sa mga programa sa workforce natin ay ang Tourism Industry Training Program.

**[01:40:05.300] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001944)  
So, it benefits 64,000 direct training beneficiaries that focuses on training courses, digitalization and technology, and certification and ladderization programs.

**[01:40:17.560] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001945)  
Yun po.

**[01:40:20.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001946)  
Yun lamang po, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, maraming salamat po to the Department of Tourism.

**[01:40:29.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001947)  
Thank you po.

**[01:40:30.400] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001948)  
Thank you very much, Majority Leader.

**[01:40:33.780] Rep. De Carlo L. Uy** (asr-001949)  
Mr. Speaker, next to interpelate the sponsor, I move to recognize Representative Sergio Dago'ok of Partylist APEC.

**[01:40:41.300] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-001950)  
The gentleman from APEC Partylist, Representative Sergio Dago'ok is recognized.

**[01:40:47.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001951)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker. Thank you, Majority Leader.

**[01:40:52.740] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001952)  
Magandang gabi po sa ating sponsor at sa pamilya ng Department of Tourism.

**[01:40:59.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001953)  
Would the Distinguished Sponsor yield to some clarificatory questions regarding the budget of the Department of Tourism?

**[01:41:08.800] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001954)  
Of course, po. Gladly.

**[01:41:11.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001955)  
Unang-una, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:41:17.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001956)  
May nakalaan tayo na 1 billion para sa branding campaign. Tama po ba?

**[01:41:26.420] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001957)  
Tama po yan.

**[01:41:28.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001958)  
So, ang budget ba na ito, mayroong mga measurable performance indicators?

**[01:41:42.640] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001959)  
Tama po yan.

**[01:41:45.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001960)  
Kandi, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:41:47.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001961)  
Distinguished Sponsor, give some specific measurable performance sa branding.

**[01:42:01.960] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001962)  
Ano po yung mga key performance indicators na tumalab at nagiging efektibo yung branding strategy natin?

**[01:42:17.020] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001963)  
Mr. Speaker, as mentioned kanina po, yung ginawa po na Love the Philippines campaign, part po ito ng ating branding campaign,

**[01:42:25.380] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001964)
